- **[morph]** @MorphNetwork (official) — 2026-05-12T12:45:09.000Z
  > Why legacy rails make reconciliation harder:

• payment data gets split across providers
• banks and processors separate movement from context
• information is inconsistent
• teams spend time stitching records back together

The money moved, and the mess came with it.

- **[morph]** @MorphNetwork (official) — 2026-05-12T12:45:06.000Z
